description
# #1 Sydney History Tour! ##Tour snapshot Get real! Skip the superficial and dive deep on this Sydney tour that reveals the bizarre backstories and incredible histories of the city's icons. Behind the froth and bubble of sexy Sydney lies the stories of the winners who made it... and losers who didn't. ##Highlights - Discover a new meaning to 'off the beaten path' as we stroll through Sydney via a hidden trail - Hear a warts and all account of the birth of modern Australia - See all of Sydney’s most popular sights from a different perspective - Unique photo opportunities along the way that only a local knows. Inclusions: - Local English-speaking guide - A digital record of our tour that includes a 3D map of where we walked and downloadable photos taken by the guide. - A digital copy of our 100% Ridgy-didge Aussie slang survival guide (Volume 1) Exclusions: Items of a personal nature, tips or gratuities for guides, meals, and bottled water. ##Schedule details Duration: 2 hours Meeting point: Foyer Marriot Hotel 30 Pitt Street Sydney Recommended Starting time: 10.00 AM Ending point: Circular Quay Sydney

#1 Sydney History Tour! Get real! Skip the superficial and dive deep on this Sydney tour that reveals the bizarre backstories and incredible histories of the city's icons. Behind the froth and bubble of sexy Sydney lies the stories of the winners who made it... and losers who didn't. - Discover a new meaning to 'off the beaten path' as we stroll through Sydney via a hidden trail - Hear a warts and all account of the birth of modern Australia - See all of Sydney’s most popular sights from a different perspective - Unique photo opportunities along the way that only a local knows. Inclusions: - Local English-speaking guide - A digital record of our tour that includes a 3D map of where we walked and downloadable photos taken by the guide. - A digital copy of our 100% Ridgy-didge Aussie slang survival guide (Volume 1) Exclusions: Items of a personal nature, tips or gratuities for guides, meals, and bottled water. Duration: 2 hours Meeting point: Foyer Marriot Hotel 30 Pitt Street Sydney Recommended Starting time: 10.00 AM Ending point: Circular Quay Sydney

cancellation_policy
Our tours run rain or shine. We only cancel tours in the event of unsafe weather conditions. Customers will receive a full refund in case we cancel due to unsafe weather conditions or other unforeseen circumstances. We reserve the right to change the route in case of unexpected circumstances. The value of the customer's transaction may be subject to taxes, duties, foreign transaction, currency exchange or other fees. Your bank or credit or debit card company may convert the payment into the local currency and charge fees, resulting in differences between the amount displayed on 24hoursinsydney.com.au, and the final amount charged to your bank account or credit or debit card statement. Please contact your bank or card company if you have any questions concerning any applicable conversion fees. Customers who wish to cancel their tour will receive a full refund with more than 24 hours notice of cancellation. Cancellations within 24 hours of the tour start time (Sydney local time) will be charged the full price unless they also purchased tour cancellation protection at the same time when purchasing the tour itself. With the tour cancellation protection, customers will receive a full refund (minus the booking and cancellation protection fee) if it is requested until the start of the tour. No refund will be provided after that. Customers wishing to reschedule their tour within 24 hours of the scheduled departure (Sydney local time), may contact us at johnuasydney@gmail.com to check our availability. Should we have places available, they'll be charged a rescheduling fee of AUD$20 per booking. Please note that 24 Hours in Sydney does not guarantee rescheduling of any tour less than 24 hours before the scheduled departure time. No refund is provided for any unused portion of a tour.

Description & Content
shortDescription
The Trails are calling, hire one of our pedal assist Giant Talon E-bikes and explore the Wambenger Trails in Collie with ease. You'll have fun, go further and enjoy the trails for longer.
description
Unlock a whole new world of adventure and find freedom on the trails, as you enjoy cruising the ups and downs of the Wambenger Trails in Collie, on one of our powerful, versatile and highly capable, Giant Talon E-bikes. Locations include: In Collie - Arklow Trail Network, Town Trails, Kylie Trail and the Munda Biddi Trail. Wellington National Park - A variety of trails in one central location. The Giant E Talon is a single suspension, hard tail, bike with 10 gears and 5 support levels, hydraulic disc brakes and 29" tyres. Please select your bike size in the extras. All bike hires come with a helmet. Sizes are to be used as a guide only. Small: 162 - 172 cm Medium: 169 - 179cm Large: 176 - 186cm Our e-bikes are suitable for fire trails and green (easy) rated mountain bike trails only, including the Munda Biddi Trail. They are not permitted to be used on Blue or Black trails. Riders must be over 16 years old and with a parent, otherwise 18 years old. All hires are required to provide a photo driver’s license, credit card details and agree to our terms and conditions. General wear and tear is acceptable but hirers will be responsible for any lost, stolen or damage of equipment during the period of hire. Day hire is 7 hours 9.00 - 4.00pm Half day is 4 hours 9.00am -1.00pm How far can I expect to ride on a single battery charge? The battery range can be from 55km -170km. The range for a single battery charge can vary greatly depending on conditions such as the combined weight of the rider and cargo; wind resistance; tire pressure and tread profile; terrain and elevation changes; road or trail surface; outdoor temperature; maintenance of the E-Bike; and the condition of the battery. We do NOT allow transportation of our bikes in vehicles.  All prices include bike hire and helmet.

Description & Content
description
Moreton Island is a unique paradise of golden beaches, rolling surf and beautiful freshwater lakes. Join Sunset Safaris as we explore and snorkel the stunning Tangalooma Shipwrecks, and see the shipwrecks in our very unique “Transparent Kayaks.” Experience the exhilaration of sliding down the giant sand dunes of “The Desert” up to 70 km/h. Moreton Island is one of the great ADVENTURE DESTINATIONS in Australia, and with Sunset Safaris you will experience the very best natural attractions this world-famous Island has to offer. Our award-nominated Tour Guides are tour professionals, with an intimate knowledge of Moreton Island’s unique attractions. We have become famous in Queensland for our love for fun, and that’s why Sunset Safaris is the “People’s Choice” tour company. Our mission is to make your visit to Moreton Island more than just a beautiful place to go, but an “Experience to Remember.”Australian Sunset Safaris are the FIRST to pioneer “Transparent Kayaks” in Queensland and around the Tangalooma Shipwrecks. See fish and coral like never before without having to go under the water. Our 1-Day Tour of Moreton Island takes in the very best activities and scenery the western side of this beautiful Island has to offer during the day. Jam packed full of fun, you can experience Sand Tobogganing, Snorkeling and swimming, 4WDing along the beach and sand tracks, Transparent Kayaking over shipwrecks and much more. This is the ULTIMATE ECO Adventure Day Tour in Queensland.

detail_description
Moreton Island is a unique paradise of golden beaches, rolling surf and beautiful freshwater lakes. Join Sunset Safaris as we explore and snorkel the stunning Tangalooma Shipwrecks, and see the shipwrecks in our very unique “Transparent Kayaks.” Experience the exhilaration of sliding down the giant sand dunes of “The Desert” up to 70 km/h. Moreton Island is one of the great ADVENTURE DESTINATIONS in Australia, and with Sunset Safaris you will experience the very best natural attractions this world-famous Island has to offer. Our award-nominated Tour Guides are tour professionals, with an intimate knowledge of Moreton Island’s unique attractions. We have become famous in Queensland for our love for fun, and that’s why Sunset Safaris is the “People’s Choice” tour company. Our mission is to make your visit to Moreton Island more than just a beautiful place to go, but an “Experience to Remember.”Australian Sunset Safaris are the FIRST to pioneer “Transparent Kayaks” in Queensland and around the Tangalooma Shipwrecks. See fish and coral like never before without having to go under the water. Our 1-Day Tour of Moreton Island takes in the very best activities and scenery the western side of this beautiful Island has to offer during the day. Jam packed full of fun, you can experience Sand Tobogganing, Snorkeling and swimming, 4WDing along the beach and sand tracks, Transparent Kayaking over shipwrecks and much more. This is the ULTIMATE ECO Adventure Day Tour in Queensland.

Booking & Cancellation
pickupNotes
Please note that during the month of April and May 2025 pick-up times will vary due to a change to the ferry schedule. Pick-up options: Gold Coast: Departs 05:45 from Mantra on View Hotel (22 View Ave, Surfers Paradise QLD 4217). April 17th and 18th 2025 pick-up will be 07:30. April 20th to 22nd and 27th 2025 pick up will be 07:00. All other dates in April pick-up will be 05:30. 5th May 2025 pick-up will be 07:15. All other dates in May 2025 pick-up is 05:30. Brisbane: Departs 07:00 Brisbane Coach Terminal, Parkland Crescent QLD 4000. April 17th and 18th 2025 pick-up will be 09:00. April 20th to 22nd and 27th 2025 pick up will be 08:30. All other dates in April pick-up will be 07:00. 5th May 2025 pick-up will be 08:45. All other dates in May 2025 pick-up is 07:00. Departs 07:30 Port of Brisbane (14 Howard Smith Dr, Port of Brisbane QLD 4178). April 17th and 18th 2025 pick-up will be 10:00. April 20th to 22nd and 27th 2025 pick up will be 09:30. All other dates in April pick-up will be 07:30. 5th May 2025 pick-up will be 09:45. All other dates in May 2025 pick-up is 07:30.
dropoffNotes
Drop-off in Brisbane at 18:30 and Gold Coast at 19:30. Return times are approximate and subject to ferry schedule and traffic conditions. Please note that during the month of April and May 2025 drop-off times will vary due to a change to the ferry schedule.
specialNotes
What to bring: Water bottle, swimwear, sunscreen, beach towel, hat, camera, flip flops/thongs, walking shoes and insect repellant. Conditions: The minimum age is 4 years. Unsuitable for toddlers, Child rates apply for 13 years and under. Suitable for people of all fitness levels. In cases of dangerous or poor weather conditions and rain, a scenic tour of the island may be conducted in place of affected activities. The itinerary is subject to times and weather conditions. Small carry-on luggage only. Small overnight bag restrictions apply(40cm wide x 30cm deep x 60cm high). No refunds for COVID No Show. Pickup and Drop off in different regions attract additional fees (POA). All prices are subject to change without notice & at the discretion of Sunset Safaris. All information stated in this publication is true & correct at the time of publication. Due to traffic, & the extreme conditions experienced on Moreton Island to buses & the environment, Sunset Safaris reserves the right to alter or cancel any section of the itinerary which may result from road, traffic, weather, mechanical, or any other condition or failure or delay of an operational nature without penalty, & such conditions will be considered as a Force Majeure Event. Sunset Safaris will take all reasonable steps to rectify the situation during any such event, however, no liability will be accepted by Sunset Safaris in circumstances outside of Sunset Safaris' control. No responsibility or liability will be taken in the event of injury from pre-existing medical conditions (eg: bad back), accident, injury, loss or damage to persons or property due to persons' own negligence. These booking conditions do not waive Sunset Safaris from any of its obligations according to law. Minimum booking numbers & cancellation fees apply. Not included: POB National Park and Ferry Fees (To be paid in cash upon departure). Per person Adult AUD $80 and Children 13 years and under AUD $60. Pick-up and Drop-off in different regions attract additional fees. Levies and Surcharges: A fuel levy may be applied if fuel rises above $2.50 per litre. A $20 AUD surcharge applies to all tours operating during Christmas/New Year & Easter Periods. $20 AUD surcharge (Cash or Credit Card) payable directly to Sunset Safaris on all gazetted holidays in Australia.

Had surgeries or broken bones within the past 3 months; or are currently under doctors supervision regarding surgery or injury, regardless of when surgery or injury occurred; An individual weight in excess of 140kg Or is: Unable to stand for one hour unassisted, Currently pregnant Less than 7 years of age or Under 120cm tall."

detail_health_safety
Fitness to Fly Terms: Hot Air Ballooning is an adventure activity that requires a moderate level of fitness and well-being in order to participate. By noting your agreement at time of booking, you are confirming that no member of your booking party has: Significant knee, hip, back or neck injury or issues; Any other existing conditions that would preclude them from following the pilots instructions; climbing aboard the balloon using the footholds cut into the side of the basket; Assuming the landing positions with knees bent when instructed to do so; Absorbing a small bump when the balloon is landing or would prevent them from holding on to the safety handles when the balloon is landing. Had surgeries or broken bones within the past 3 months; or are currently under doctors supervision regarding surgery or injury, regardless of when surgery or injury occurred; An individual weight in excess of 140kg Or is: Unable to stand for one hour unassisted, Currently pregnant Less than 7 years of age or Under 120cm tall.

Description & Content
description
#Sequinned Steps - LGBT Sydney ##Tour snapshot Sydney's world-famous Mardi Gras is the evolution of over 200 years of gay and lesbian history in this city. From the gay governor whose conduct scandalised the British Empire to the bright lights of Oxford Street, hear the stories and see the sites that make Sydney one of the friendliest cities for gay and lesbian culture in the world. Your guide brings the hidden history to life, one laneway and landmark at a time. ##Highlights * Uncover the hidden tales of key gay and lesbian figures who shaped the Sydney landscape * Walk Oxford Street, the traditional heart of Sydney's gay and lesbian scene * Learn the story behind the world-famous Sydney Gay and Lesbian Mardi Gras * Finish with a drink (additional cost) at an iconic gay hangout, and you might just share a table with a drag queen Inclusions: Local English-speaking guide, choice of alcoholic/non-alcoholic drink (additional cost) at the conclusion of the tour. Exclusions: Additional food and drink, souvenirs and items of a personal nature, tips or gratuities for your guide. ##Schedule details Duration: 2 hours Meeting point: Hyde Park Barracks Piazza (Queens Square, 12 Macquarie Street, Sydney) Recommended Starting time: 2.00 PM Ending point: Kinselas Hotel
description_safe_html
Sequinned Steps - LGBT Sydney Tour snapshot Sydney's world-famous Mardi Gras is the evolution of over 200 years of gay and lesbian history in this city. From the gay governor whose conduct scandalised the British Empire to the bright lights of Oxford Street, hear the stories and see the sites that make Sydney one of the friendliest cities for gay and lesbian culture in the world. Your guide brings the hidden history to life, one laneway and landmark at a time. Highlights Uncover the hidden tales of key gay and lesbian figures who shaped the Sydney landscape Walk Oxford Street, the traditional heart of Sydney's gay and lesbian scene Learn the story behind the world-famous Sydney Gay and Lesbian Mardi Gras Finish with a drink (additional cost) at an iconic gay hangout, and you might just share a table with a drag queen Inclusions: Local English-speaking guide, choice of alcoholic/non-alcoholic drink (additional cost) at the conclusion of the tour. detail_description
Sequinned Steps - LGBT Sydney Sydney's world-famous Mardi Gras is the evolution of over 200 years of gay and lesbian history in this city. From the gay governor whose conduct scandalised the British Empire to the bright lights of Oxford Street, hear the stories and see the sites that make Sydney one of the friendliest cities for gay and lesbian culture in the world. Your guide brings the hidden history to life, one laneway and landmark at a time. Uncover the hidden tales of key gay and lesbian figures who shaped the Sydney landscape Walk Oxford Street, the traditional heart of Sydney's gay and lesbian scene Learn the story behind the world-famous Sydney Gay and Lesbian Mardi Gras Finish with a drink (additional cost) at an iconic gay hangout, and you might just share a table with a drag queen Inclusions: Local English-speaking guide, choice of alcoholic/non-alcoholic drink (additional cost) at the conclusion of the tour. Exclusions: Additional food and drink, souvenirs and items of a personal nature, tips or gratuities for your guide. Duration: 2 hours Meeting point: Hyde Park Barracks Piazza (Queens Square, 12 Macquarie Street, Sydney) Recommended Starting time: 2.00 PM Ending point: Kinselas Hotel

cancellation_policy
Our tours run rain or shine. We only cancel tours in the event of unsafe weather conditions. Customers will receive a full refund in case we cancel due to unsafe weather conditions or other unforeseen circumstances. We reserve the right to change the route in case of unexpected circumstances. The value of the customer's transaction may be subject to taxes, duties, foreign transaction, currency exchange or other fees. Your bank or credit or debit card company may convert the payment into the local currency and charge fees, resulting in differences between the amount displayed on 24hoursinsydney.com.au, and the final amount charged to your bank account or credit or debit card statement. Please contact your bank or card company if you have any questions concerning any applicable conversion fees. Customers who wish to cancel their tour will receive a full refund with more than 24 hours notice of cancellation. Cancellations within 24 hours of the tour start time (Sydney local time) will be charged the full price unless they also purchased tour cancellation protection at the same time when purchasing the tour itself. With the tour cancellation protection, customers will receive a full refund (minus the booking and cancellation protection fee) if it is requested until the start of the tour. No refund will be provided after that. Customers wishing to reschedule their tour within 24 hours of the scheduled departure (Sydney local time), may contact us at johnuasydney@gmail.com to check our availability. Should we have places available, they'll be charged a rescheduling fee of AUD$20 per booking. Please note that 24 Hours in Sydney does not guarantee rescheduling of any tour less than 24 hours before the scheduled departure time. No refund is provided for any unused portion of a tour.

Description & Content
description
Join Tropic Wings for a full day of epic adventure to the Daintree Rainforest and Cape Tribulation and fall in love with Tropical North Queensland’s most iconic locations. Your day will be filled with memorable experiences, local knowledge and Instagram-worthy views, all while knowing that you will be offsetting your carbon emissions on this premium eco-accredited tour. Starting with the scenic Great Barrier Reef Drive, recently voted as one of the most iconic road trips in Australia, you will make your way up to Port Douglas for your first stop at the award-winning Wildlife Habitat. Get to know the local wildlife on an immersive guided experience with one of our knowledgeable Wildlife Keepers. You will get the chance to hand-feed animals such as the elusive Cassowary which is notoriously hard to spot in the wild! Be a Wildlife Keeper for the morning with this money-can’t-buy experience that is exclusive to your tour. You can also purchase a photo experience with one of our koalas to remember the moment. Cross the Daintree River on the cable ferry and travel north to the World Heritage Daintree Rainforest where you will enjoy a dip in the pristine waters of Mason’s freshwater swimming hole. Followed by a locally sourced lunch at Masons Café. The Mason family settled in Cape Tribulation in 1932 and is now run by third-generation Lawrence Mason. Explore the world’s oldest rainforest with one of our knowledgeable local guides. Learn about the fascinating flora and fauna that call the Daintree Rainforest home on a boardwalk tour curated to your group’s interests. Visit Cape Tribulation Beach and see the only place in the world where two world heritage sites meet: the World Heritage-listed Wet Tropics Rainforest and the Great Barrier Reef. After a big day of adventure, the perfect way to cool down and relax is with an exotic tasting experience at Daintree Ice Cream Company. Handmade on site with organically grown fruit it is easy to see why this ice cream has won multiple awards. Head to Alexandra Lookout for a photo opportunity while you enjoy beautiful views of the Daintree Rainforest and the Coral Sea. Board a Daintree River cruise and search for wildlife including wild Estuarine Crocodiles and birds in the unique mangrove ecosystem.

detail_description
Join Tropic Wings for a full day of epic adventure to the Daintree Rainforest and Cape Tribulation and fall in love with Tropical North Queensland’s most iconic locations. Your day will be filled with memorable experiences, local knowledge and Instagram-worthy views, all while knowing that you will be offsetting your carbon emissions on this premium eco-accredited tour. Starting with the scenic Great Barrier Reef Drive, recently voted as one of the most iconic road trips in Australia, you will make your way up to Port Douglas for your first stop at the award-winning Wildlife Habitat. Get to know the local wildlife on an immersive guided experience with one of our knowledgeable Wildlife Keepers. You will get the chance to hand-feed animals such as the elusive Cassowary which is notoriously hard to spot in the wild! Be a Wildlife Keeper for the morning with this money-can’t-buy experience that is exclusive to your tour. You can also purchase a photo experience with one of our koalas to remember the moment. Cross the Daintree River on the cable ferry and travel north to the World Heritage Daintree Rainforest where you will enjoy a dip in the pristine waters of Mason’s freshwater swimming hole. Followed by a locally sourced lunch at Masons Café. The Mason family settled in Cape Tribulation in 1932 and is now run by third-generation Lawrence Mason. Explore the world’s oldest rainforest with one of our knowledgeable local guides. Learn about the fascinating flora and fauna that call the Daintree Rainforest home on a boardwalk tour curated to your group’s interests. Visit Cape Tribulation Beach and see the only place in the world where two world heritage sites meet: the World Heritage-listed Wet Tropics Rainforest and the Great Barrier Reef. After a big day of adventure, the perfect way to cool down and relax is with an exotic tasting experience at Daintree Ice Cream Company. Handmade on site with organically grown fruit it is easy to see why this ice cream has won multiple awards. Head to Alexandra Lookout for a photo opportunity while you enjoy beautiful views of the Daintree Rainforest and the Coral Sea. Board a Daintree River cruise and search for wildlife including wild Estuarine Crocodiles and birds in the unique mangrove ecosystem.
detail_highlights

specialNotes
What to bring: Your voucher (if you have one), camera, hat, sunscreen & insect repellent, comfortable walking shoes, a bottle of water, money for drinks and snacks. Swimmers, towel, dry change of clothes & a light jumper in winter months. Conditions: The minimum age is 4 years. Child rates apply for 4-14 years. Children under the age of 16 are not permitted to travel unless accompanied by an adult. Please contact us at least 24 hours prior to your travel date if your pick-up location has not been confirmed at the time of booking. For Bookings within 24 hours of travel, please ring 07 40419400 to confirm the booking. Passengers are required to call 48 hours prior to travel to confirm accommodation details and pick-up time. Itineraries may operate in reverse or in a different sequence. Transfers from Cairns city are from centralized locations. Transfers not available from private residences (including AirBnB properties) – guests will need to make their own way to another pickup location. A moderate level of fitness is required for this tour. If you have any mobility impairments or may require further assistance on the day of the tour, please inform us in advance.

description
Add a passenger to your jetski for $59.00! A passenger can only be added to your booking along with a full-price jetski. This is an add-on option to the tour and can not be purchased on its own. Maximum 1 additional passenger per jetski permitted. Passengers must be 8 years of age or older. Please disregard the 5:30am start time and bring the passenger along to the tour you have booked the jet ski on.

Booking & Cancellation
terms
Terms and Conditions You must meet your guide outside Bella Restaurant (f3/19 Kitchener Dr, Darwin City 0800) at least 30 minutes prior to your tour start time. Customer Requirements: It is mandatory that all customers must read, write and understand English to be eligible to participate in our tours. Customers are not allowed to be under the influence of DRUGS or ALCOHOL at any time while on 00seven Jet Skis. 00Seven Jet Ski Adventures provide a comprehensive safety briefing which is mandatory for all participants to view and understand. All participants must complete the accompanying test prior to departure on any of our tours. Participants can watch the pre-tour safety briefing here - https://youtu.be/Z65H5WcCLj0 Age Restrictions: Passengers 10 to 15 can ride if they are accompanied by a driver 18 years or older. Teenagers aged 12 to 15 may have the opportunity to drive the ski when accompanied by an adult 18 or over at the discretion of the tour guide. Teenagers aged 16 to 18 can ride the ski alone providing they have a parent or guardian’s authorization. Weight Restrictions: Not to exceed 200 kg including rider and passenger combined. Time, Distance and Routes: Please note that the time and distance of our Tours may vary depending on numerous factors such as weather conditions, water conditions, rider ability and traffic conditions. Depending on the time of the year (peak or off-peak) and tidal movements, the routes of our Tours may also change. Cancellation Policy: Cancellations by a customer: 00Seven Jet Ski Adventures have a cancellation policy which requires a minimum of 24 hours’ notice prior to departure date. · Within 24 hours – no refund and reschedule not permitted · 48 hrs - 24 hrs – 50% cancellation fee, reschedule permitted · 72 hrs – 48 hrs – 25% cancellation fee, reschedule permitted · 72 hrs or over – 15% cancellation fee, reschedule permitted Bookings may be rescheduled subject to availability. All group bookings require a 7-day notice prior to departure for cancellation. Cancellation by 00Seven Jet Ski or booking agent: Should it be necessary for 00Seven Jet Ski to cancel due to circumstances beyond their control, a full refund will be made to the customer if rescheduling is not possible. Failure to turn up to your tour on time or not at all will result in you losing your booking in full and no rescheduling will be available without full payment of new booking. ﻿﻿﻿ Weather: 00Seven Jet Ski Adventures operate in most weather conditions. Should un-favourable conditions be imminent due to storms or extreme conditions all reasonable steps will be taken to re schedule the booking. Should this not be possible a full refund will be given to our customers. Liability: The rider of the Jet ski is liable for the first $5000 in repairs for any damage caused to the machine due to collision, deviation off coarse set by the tour guide or any other careless actions. Please respect the guide and the directions given on tour. You are responsible for the ski you are riding. Gift Vouchers: All gift vouchers are non-refundable, not transferable and not redeemable for cash. Once a booking is made using a gift voucher or a voucher from a booking agent, the same cancellation policy applies as above. Gift Vouchers will not be refunded under any circumstances. If due process of 48-hour notice has not been made the gift voucher will be forfeited.
additionalInformation
Thank you for booking your adventure with 00seven! We are looking forward to meeting you at Bella Restaurant at the Waterfront (located beside Wharf One Restaurant) 30 minutes prior to your tour start time.  Attached is a map to the meeting point. If you are lost, please give us a call at 0427 007 000. See you soon, Bond. - The team at 00seven

Description & Content
shortDescription
Witness the sensational glow rise above the city and escape reality on this 90 minute mission. Slingshot your way along the coastline, carving sharp lines through the ocean. No better way to start your day! Departs at 7 AM. Join Mr Bond.
description
Departing at 7am (April to September) and 6.30am (October to December) Witness the sensational glow rise above the city and escape reality on this 90 minute mission. Slingshot your way along the coastline, carving sharp lines through the ocean. You often find conditions as smooth as a martini glass at this time of day, this experience will have you gasping for more! Price is based on 1 adult per jetski and addtional passengers are $59, maximum 1 passenger per ski. Passengers must be 8 years of age or older. Passengers 16 and over may switch freely with the driver of the jet ski throughout the tour. See "Age Restrictions" below for additional passenger information. Not totally comfortable in that drivers seat? Hop on the back of our guide's jetski and enjoy the ride for just $75! Maximum 1 guide passenger per tour is permitted. Passengers must be 8 years of age or older. Terms and Conditions Liability: The driver of the Jetski is responsible for controlling their jetski in a safe manner.   By signing the waiver prior to your tour, you agree that your Credit Card details will be used to recover up to $5000 required to repair the damaged equipment. Option  Adventures provides customers the opportunity to purchase Insurance cover for an additional $25 which covers DAMAGE ONLY for the first $2500 caused by the customer.  Please respect the guide and the directions given on tour. You are responsible for the ski you are riding. Please note, no spins - 360's, attempting to spray other Jet skis with or deliberately trying to throw off passengers.  You must meet your guide at Dock 1, Stokes Hill Wharf 30 minutes prior to your tour start time. Customer Requirements: It is mandatory that all customers must read, write and understand English to be eligible to participate in our tours. Customers are not allowed to be under the influence of DRUGS or ALCOHOL at any time while on 00seven Jet Skis. 00Seven Jet Ski Adventures provide a comprehensive safety briefing which is mandatory for all participants to view and understand. All participants must complete the accompanying test prior to departure on any of our tours. Participants can watch the pre-tour safety briefing here - https://youtu.be/Z65H5WcCLj0 Age Restrictions: Passengers 8 to 15 can ride if they are accompanied by a driver 18 years or older. Teenagers aged 12 to 15 may have the opportunity to drive the ski when accompanied by an adult 18 or over at the discretion of the tour guide. Teenagers aged 16 to 18 can ride the ski alone providing they have a parent or guardian’s authorization. Weight Restrictions: Not to exceed 200 kg including rider and passenger combined. Time, Distance and Routes: Please note that the time and distance of our Tours may vary depending on numerous factors such as weather conditions, water conditions, rider ability and traffic conditions. Depending on the time of the year (peak or off-peak) and tidal movements, the routes of our Tours may also change. Cancellation Policy: Cancellations by a customer: 00Seven Jet Ski Adventures have a cancellation policy which requires a minimum of 24 hours’ notice prior to departure date. Bookings may be rescheduled subject to availability. All group bookings require a 7-day notice prior to departure for cancellation. Cancellation by 00Seven Jet Ski or booking agent: Should it be necessary for 00Seven Jet Ski to cancel due to circumstances beyond their control, a full refund will be made to the customer if rescheduling is not possible. Failure to turn up to your tour on time or not at all will result in you losing your booking in full and no rescheduling will be available without full payment of new booking.  Weather: 00Seven Jet Ski Adventures operate in most weather conditions. Should un-favourable conditions be imminent due to storms or extreme conditions all reasonable steps will be taken to re schedule the booking. Should this not be possible a full refund will be given to our customers.

Teenagers aged 16 to 18 can ride the ski alone providing they have a parent or guardian’s authorization. Weight Restrictions: Not to exceed 200 kg including rider and passenger combined. Time, Distance and Routes: Please note that the time and distance of our Tours may vary depending on numerous factors such as weather conditions, water conditions, rider ability and traffic conditions. Depending on the time of the year (peak or off-peak) and tidal movements, the routes of our Tours may also change. Cancellation Policy: Cancellations by a customer: 00Seven Jet Ski Adventures have a cancellation policy which requires a minimum of 24 hours’ notice prior to departure date. · Within 24 hours – no refund and reschedule not permitted · 48 hrs - 24 hrs – 50% cancellation fee, reschedule permitted · 72 hrs – 48 hrs – 25% cancellation fee, reschedule permitted · 72 hrs or over – 15% cancellation fee, reschedule permitted Bookings may be rescheduled subject to availability. All group bookings require a 7-day notice prior to departure for cancellation. Cancellation by 00Seven Jet Ski or booking agent: Should it be necessary for 00Seven Jet Ski to cancel due to circumstances beyond their control, a full refund will be made to the customer if rescheduling is not possible. Failure to turn up to your tour on time or not at all will result in you losing your booking in full and no rescheduling will be available without full payment of new booking. ﻿﻿﻿ Weather: 00Seven Jet Ski Adventures operate in most weather conditions. Should un-favourable conditions be imminent due to storms or extreme conditions all reasonable steps will be taken to re schedule the booking. Should this not be possible a full refund will be given to our customers. Liability: The rider of the Jet ski is liable for the first $5000 in repairs for any damage caused to the machine due to collision, deviation off coarse set by the tour guide or any other careless actions. Please respect the guide and the directions given on tour. You are responsible for the ski you are riding.

Description & Content
shortDescription
Are you up to the challenge? Buckle up and leave the crowds behind as we take you into your own tropical paradise, landing on the beach where you will be left to be a Castaway! Come and ride our ex U.S Military amphibious LARC .
description
You are left to "survive" on the island for 2days/1nts with only a handful of fellow thrill seekers and an array of native wildlife. The hard work is already done for you with roomy camp tents already setup, mattresses, solar lights, usb power, camp kitchen, eskies and a toilet.  With only a camp supervisor to ensure the smooth running of the tour, there is no-one planning your stay or looking after you...you are free to do as you please and are responsible for your own safety and your own fun! Try kayaking, explore caves, go fishing or crabbing with your local Aussie Bushman, sand board down 35m sand dunes into the water, zoom about in motorised boats, snorkel, find oysters, explore the natural wonders of this isolated island...or better still, just relax, lie back on the beach and do nothing! At the end of a big day, what better way to relax than by watching the glorious sunset over the western side of the island whilst enjoying our famous Castaway camp fire stew! Spend the evening under the amazing stars, around the camp fire with new friends playing camp fire games and singing songs.  This is the opportunity of a lifetime...and we guarantee you'll remember it! Tour price includes: Return transport, transfers to/from Caltex Petrol Station in Agnes Water & 1770 Marina, camping for two nights, tents, mattresses, fully set up camp kitchen, lights, USB power, toilet, eskies, water, maps, first aid kit in camp, Aussie bushman to demonstrate survival skills and a cooked evening meal (additional compulsory cost of $5/nt).  Activities include: Use of motorised boats, sand boarding down 35m sand dunes into the water, kayaking, fishing, crabbing, snorkeling, swimming, exploring, beach games & more. Extra nights available (subject to availability, min 2nt blocks) please enquire. 15 years and under are classed as a child 4 under free

terms
CANCELLATIONS NO refunds 7days of departure date. Gift Vouchers are non refundable. DATE CHANGES NO changes 7days from departure date (no refunds after a date change has occurred). TOUR DEPARTURES / RECONFIRMATIONS Tours depart and return from the Greyhound Bus Stop in Agnes Water QLD. Passengers need to make their own way to/from here. We then transfer you to/from the 1770 Marina. Passengers MUST text/call 0401 770 572 to reconfirm their attendance and obtain the pre-trip briefing time at least 24-48hrs prior to their date of travel.

Description & Content
shortDescription
An amphibious adventure for all ages! A fun filled day trip along north of the Town of 1770 to the Bustard Head Lightstation. Guided tour inside the lighthouse tower, morning tea, lunch and the thrill of sandboarding are all included.
description
This full day LARC tour will take you on a journey into history and over vast sandy beaches and crystal clear waters north of the Town of 1770. With National Parks to the left and the Great Barrier Reef Marine Park to the right, the amphibious vessel traverses four tidal creeks, to arrive at a scenic and shaded picnic area for a morning tea of cake, biscuits, fresh fruit and cold refreshments. The LARC then continues the picturesque 4WD journey up Bustard Headland to the Bustard Head Lightstation - Queensland's first coastal lighthouse, standing 19 metres tall. The cast iron lighthouse tower was built in 1868 and automated in 1986, and in 2013 became Queensland's only operating lighthouse open for tours inside. Upon arrival at the lightstation you can climb the original stairs to the balcony of the lighthouse and hear tales of triumph and turmoil from our experienced guides. Absorb the history inside the tower and the restored head light-keepers cottage, while sharing an interpretative dialogue about the large collection of lighthouse artefacts within. Just beyond the lighthouse is a short winding path leading to a quaint and well tended graveyard, peacefully nestled in beautiful bush surroundings.  The panoramic views of Bustard Bay and its pristine surroundings will have you in awe. Morning tea, picnic lunch, cold drinks and billy tea or coffee brewed over an open campfire are included in your day tour. The 35 metre sand dunes of Middle Island are an ideal location for sandboarding fun, so the amphibian will deliver you to the base and our guides will provide a lesson, before sending you up the dune and setting you free to glide down! You can choose to stay dry or if you're feeling a little more adventurous why not skim right off the dunes splashing and skipping across the tidal water. If you would prefer not to participate, sandboarding is a great spectator sport.  As you are transported back to the 1770 Marina in true beach buggy style, you can soak up the picturesque colours of the sky and the wildlife in the shallows and in the coastal bush. Join us for this 7 hour guided tour of LARCing fun!
durationMinutes
1200
